SMS) |\n    31→\n    32→### Technology Components\n    33→| Component | Type | Data Flow Role |\n    34→|-----------|------|---------------|\n    35→| Landing Page | Public Web | Entry point for 38→| Tavus Video AI | Video Interaction | Provides immersive two-way video engagement |\n    39→| TextMagic SMS | Messaging Gateway | Enables two-way text communication |\n    40→| VIN Solutions | CRM (System of Record) | Central repository for all customer data |\n    41→| Communication Agent | Internal AI | Assists Sales with drafting and context |\n    42→| Service Agent | Internal AI | Assists Service with scheduling and pricing |\n    43→| CRM Guru Agent | Query AI | Natural language interface for VIN data |\n    44→| Trigger Engine | Automation | Executes logic-based outbound actions |\n    45→| Kill Switch | Compliance Control | Master override for all outbound communications |\n    46→| TeamBox | Unified Inbox | Central UI for all staff-customer messaging |\n    47→| Metrics Dashboard | Analytics | Visualizes performance data by RBAC role |\n    48→\n    49→### Transaction Types\n    50→| Transaction Type | Flow | Key Data Captured |\n    51→|-----------------|------|-------------------|\n    52→| Widget Lead Capture | Widget -> Local DB -> VIN Solutions | Contact Info, Intent, Source URL |\n    53→| VAPI Call | VAPI -> Local DB -> VIN Solutions + TeamBox | Audio, Transcript, Sentiment, Phone # |\n    54→| Tavus Video Session | Tavus -> Local DB -> Calendar + VIN Solutions | Video ID, Session Duration, Notes |\n    55→| Outbound Trigger | Trigger -> TextMagic -> SMS -> TeamBox | Message Content, Timestamp, Status |\n    56→| Two-Way SMS | TeamBox -> Staff -> TextMagic -> Customer | Thread History, Read Status, Agent ID |\n    57→| Service Campaign | Campaign -> Trigger -> SMS -> Appointment | Campaign ID, Conversion Rate, Appt Date |\n    58→| Metric Request | VIN API -> Local Cache -> Dashboard | Aggregated Counts, Dollar Values |\n    59→| Agent Query | User -> CRM Guru -> VIN API -> AI Response | Query Text, Retrieved Records, AI Answer |\n    60→\n    61→---\n    62→\n    63→## Sales Lead Capture (US-001 through US-008)\n    64→\n    65→### US-001: Web Chat to VIN Lead Creation\n    66→**As a** Prospect **I want to** inquire about a vehicle via the website chat widget **So that** I can get information quickly without a phone call\n    67→\n    68→A prospect lands on the dealership landing page and engages the Universal Widget, selecting \"Web Chat.\" They inquire about the availability of a 2024 SUV. The AI collects their name, phone number, and specific vehicle interest. A new lead is immediately posted to VIN Solutions via API with the source marked as \"Nexxus Web Chat.\" The chat transcript is appended to the CRM notes. A Salesperson receives a notification in TeamBox.\n    69→\n    70→**Tech Stack:** Landing Page, Universal Widget (Web Chat), VIN Solutions API, TeamBox\n    71→**Success Criteria:** Lead appears in VIN Solutions within 60 seconds. Source correctly attributed to \"Nexxus Web Chat\". Salesperson can view transcript in TeamBox.\n    72→**RBAC:** Salesperson, Sales Manager\n    73→\n    74→### US-002: Tavus Video Lead\n    75→**As a** Hot Lead **I want to** interact with a video avatar to book a test drive **So that** I can have a more personalized experience remotely\n    76→\n    77→A buyer clicks the \"Video\" option on the widget. A Tavus video session initializes where an AI avatar guides them through booking a VIP test drive. The system records the session transcript and duration. It creates a \"Test Drive\" appointment in the local calendar and pushes a lead update to VIN Solutions.\n    78→\n    79→**Tech Stack:** Universal Widget (Video), Tavus Video AI, Calendar System, VIN Solutions API\n    80→**Success Criteria:** Tavus session initiates successfully. Appointment appears on internal calendar. Lead in VIN Solutions reflects video source.\n    81→**RBAC:** Sales Manager, Salesperson\n    82→\n    83→### US-003: Form Submission to Two-Way SMS\n    84→**As a** Prospect **I want to** fill out a \"Get e-Price\" form **So that** I can receive pricing details via text\n    85→\n    86→The prospect fills out a simple form on the widget requesting pricing. This creates a lead in VIN Solutions and triggers an automated workflow. The Trigger Engine fires an automated SMS via TextMagic. The prospect replies, and the reply routes directly into TeamBox, opening a two-way conversation thread where the salesperson takes over manually.\n    87→\n    88→**Tech Stack:** Universal Widget (Form), Trigger Engine, TextMagic SMS, TeamBox\n    89→**Success Criteria:** Auto-response sent within 1 minute of submission. Customer reply appears in TeamBox thread. Conversation context is preserved.\n    90→**RBAC:** Salesperson\n    91→\n    92→### US-004: VAPI Inbound Call Handling\n    93→**As a** Mobile Prospect **I want to** call the dealership directly from the website **So that** I can speak to someone immediately\n    94→\n    95→A prospect clicks \"Call Us.\" VAPI Voice AI answers, collecting contact info and intent. The salesperson reviews the transcript in TeamBox and initiates follow-up.\n    96→\n    97→**Tech Stack:** VAPI Voice AI, VIN Solutions API, TeamBox\n    98→**Success Criteria:** VAPI accurately transcribes conversation. Lead created in VIN with phone number. Salesperson notified of completed call.\n    99→**RBAC:** Salesperson\n   100→\n   101→### US-005: Walk-In Manual Entry & Auto-Followup\n   102→**As a** Salesperson **I want to** manually enter a walk-in customer's info **So that** they receive an automated thank you message\n   103→\n   104→A customer walks onto the lot. AUTHENTICATION & SECURITY\n\n**AU: Auth System**\n\n| Element | File | Type | Implementation |\n|---------|------|------|-----------------|\n| JWT Authentication | server/auth.ts | Token | HS256 with 1-hour access tokens + 7-day refresh tokens |\n| Refresh Token Storage | server/auth.ts | Cookie | HttpOnly, Secure, SameSite=Strict cookie |\n| Password Hashing | server/routes/auth.ts | Crypto | bcrypt with salting |\n| Token Verification | server/auth.ts | Middleware | JWT signature + type validation |\n| Rate Limiting | server/routes/auth.ts | Middleware | 100 per 15 minutes for auth endpoints |\n| Global Rate Limiting | server/index.ts | Middleware | 100 requests/minute on /api/* |\n\n**AU: RBAC System**\n\n| Role | Level | Permissions |\n|------|-------|-------------|\n| Super Admin | 1 | All system capabilities, all orgs |\n| Partner Admin | 2 | Multi-store management, integrations, provisioning |\n| Org Admin | 3 | Full org control (users, campaigns, agents, settings) |\n| Sales Manager | 4 | Team management, campaign execution, metric access |\n| Salesperson | 5 | Read-only campaigns, conversations, lead access |\n| Service Advisor | 5 | Service campaigns, scheduling, customer conversations |\n\n**AU: Protected Routes**\n\n| Route Pattern | Min Role | Entitlements | Description |\n|---------------|----------|--------------|-------------|\n| `/api/users` | Level 3 | - | User management (create, list, invite) |\n| `/api/agents` | Level 3 | agent_slots | Agent creation |\n| `/api/campaigns` | Level 3 | campaign_slots | Campaign creation and execution |\n| `/api/campaigns/:id/execute` | Level 3 | - | Campaign launch |\n| `/api/widgets` | Level 3 | widget_slots | Widget deployment |\n| `/api/organizations` | Level 2 | - | Multi-org provisioning |\n| `/api/billing/*` | Level 3 | - | Billing and plans |\n| `/api/documents` | - | - | Knowledge management |\n\n**AU: Middleware**\n\n| Middleware | File | Purpose |\n|----------|------|---------|\n| authenticateToken | server/auth.ts | Verify JWT and populate req.user |\n| requireRole(level) | server/auth.ts | Enforce minimum role level |\n| requireEntitlement(feature) | server/middleware/entitlementCheck.ts | Check billing feature access |\n| CORS Handling | server/index.ts | Allow configured origins |\n| Helmet | server/index.ts | Security headers (CSP, HSTS, etc.) |\n| Cookie Parser | server/index.ts | Parse refresh token cookies |\n\n**AU: Security Headers**\n\n| Header | Configuration |\n|--------|---------------|\n| Content-Security-Policy | default-src 'self', script-src 'self', style-src 'self' + fonts |\n| X-Frame-Options | DENY (via Helmet) |\n| X-Content-Type-Options | nosniff (via Helmet) |\n| CORS Origin | App base URL + localhost, widget endpoints allow * |\n\n---\n\n### INFRASTRUCTURE & CONFIGURATION\n\n**IN: Build & Development**\n\n| Config | File | Description |\n|--------|------|-------------|\n| TypeScript Build |
